| `EV-R02` | Platform Engineering / I0 | as currently governed | everything |

**No owner or date was fabricated.**


## SECTION AN — PRODUCT EXPERIENCE BENCHMARK V2 — added 2026-09-25 (report `266`)

**Benchmark V2 is the canonical Product Experience quality standard for all future execution.** It is referenced
here, never duplicated: the canonical text lives in report `266` and the audit chain it cites. Its scope covers the
Experience Principles V2, typography standard, information-hierarchy tiers, page archetypes, composition system,
surface grammar, data-visualisation grammar, KORA Index presentation, decision/action pattern, the 5s/30s/2min
scanability standard, mobile priority model, navigation standard, content standard, interaction grammar, motion
doctrine, the seven-state model, the Logo-Off Test, the craft standard, the premium-moment standard, accessibility,
the enforcement model and **Gates A–I**.

**`KORA-WP-125` is NOT invalidated.** It remains historically **COMPLETE** and its foundation is retained in full —
palette, L0/L1/L2 surface ladder, application shell, doctrine navigation grouping, icon family, accessibility
foundations, responsive shell model, terminology and copy register. **Benchmark V2 supersedes only its former final
QUALITY BAR**, never its delivery.

### AN.1 — Calibrated thresholds, from the real-Product audit

| Threshold | Ratified value |
|---|---|
| Page length — **Executive Judgment (Cockpit subtype)** | warn ≈ **2,500px** |
| Page length — **Executive Judgment (Report/Export subtype)** | warn ≈ **4,000px** — `/company/reports` legitimately carries board-pack, CSR and privacy content. **A subtype, not a seventh archetype** |
| Mobile desktop→mobile length ratio (Executive) | **acceptable ≤ 1.35× · warning > 1.35× and ≤ 1.6× · fail > 1.6×** — replaces the provisional ≤1.15×. **A detector, never a target: padding a desktop page to improve the ratio is a benchmark violation** |
| Premium moments per persona | **Retained unchanged.** Worker and Partner validation is **future applicable evidence**, not a relaxation |

### AN.2 — Successor semantics — three COMPLETE packages, none reopened

| Package | Status | Successor relationship |
|---|---|---|
| Package `KORA-WP-073` | **COMPLETE, not reopened** | Historically owns canonical IA and navigation **semantics** (per `125`'s own Out of Scope). Benchmark V2 persona **rationalisation** is successor work: Company → `143`, Admin → `127`, Worker → `129`, Partner → `130` post-capability, Advisor → existing Advisor work; **rules and enforcement → `126`**. **No new global navigation package exists.** |
| Package `KORA-WP-088` | **COMPLETE, not reopened** | Owns responsive **correctness** and overflow. Mobile **information priority** is successor work owned by `141`, persona remediation and `126` enforcement |
| Package `KORA-WP-125` | **COMPLETE, not reopened** | Foundation retained (above); quality bar superseded |

### AN.3 — Demonstrator contract — binding

A system foundation package **may not be accepted against a component library, Storybook or unit tests alone.**

| Wave | Demonstrators |
|---|---|
| **W1** — `139`, `140` | `/company/reports` · `/advisor/companies/[id]` |
| **W2** — `141`, `142` | `/company/kora-index` · `/company/reports` |

**`/company` is deliberately excluded as an early demonstrator** — its redesign depends on all four mature systems
plus an information-architecture decision, and is owned by `143`. **Demonstrator migration must not silently become
full persona remediation.**

### AN.4 — Migration and lint phases — binding

`SYSTEM → PRIMITIVES → DEMONSTRATORS → PERSONA MIGRATION → RESIDUAL SWEEP → HARDENING`,
with lint `OFF → OFF → WARN → WARN → WARN → BLOCK`.

**No foundation package may contract a flag-day rewrite** of the inline typography or inline spacing population.
Product-wide drift elimination and lint BLOCK are **programme Definition of Done, not package acceptance**.

**AMENDED 2026-09-25 (report `268` code truth at `ac91cfa`).** The figures this section originally cited —
~~a flag-day rewrite of the 2,088 inline typography or 1,761 inline spacing decisions~~ — were **scope-limited to
`app/`**, and remain correct as that historical `app/`-scope audit reading. They understate the canonical Product UI
surface. Product-wide totals at `ac91cfa`: **inline typography 2,601** (`app/` 2,088 plus `components/` 513) and
**inline spacing 2,277** (`app/` 1,824 plus `components/` 453), with **token adoption 0 on both axes**. Adjacent
typographic drift counted by neither original figure: `fontWeight` 1,452, `lineHeight` 693, `letterSpacing` 588. The
`KORA-WP-139` lint must handle **five value forms**, not one — bare number 1,882, quoted px 557, quoted rem 150, other
string 6, expression 6; a rule matching only quoted px literals would miss 72% of the population. **None of these
figures is an acceptance quota.** They size the staged migration and nothing else.

### AN.5 — Execution waves — sequencing overlays, never hard edges

**W1** `139` ‖ `140` (parallel roots) → **W2** `141` ‖ `142` → **W3** `126` amended → **W4** persona: `143`, `127`,
`128`, `129`, Advisor refine (parallel where file collision permits) → **W5** `131` → **Wπ** `130` after `051`–`059`.

**W1 UPDATE 2026-09-25.** `KORA-WP-140` is **COMPLETE** (report `269`). `KORA-WP-139` remains **READY** and is
unaffected: the two were ratified as independent DAG roots and `140` created no edge to `139`. W1 closes when `139` closes.
`141`, `142`, `143` and `126` all still depend on `139` and therefore remain BLOCKED — `140`'s completion unblocks nothing.

**W1 CLOSED 2026-09-26 (report `270`).** `KORA-WP-139` is **COMPLETE**. Both W1 roots are closed, and the
mechanical consequence is exactly the one the DAG predicted: **`141` and `142` move BLOCKED → READY**, because `139` was the
last unmet Hard Dep of each. **`126` and `143` stay BLOCKED** — both additionally depend on `141` and `142`. **W2 is now
executable: `141` ‖ `142`.** Neither is started by that closure.

- **`KORA-WP-138`** — ~~still **READY**~~ **COMPLETE 2026-09-25 (report `267`).** **Overlay SATISFIED:** its
  validated candidate `ac91cfa31e0bcad85071d467c9a5611a4668c29b` reached the canonical integration line, proven by

  concurrently. Overlay only.
- **Partner** Product Experience (`130`) remains behind `051`–`059`. Unchanged.

### AN.7 — Deferred functional items — no packages allocated

Recommendation acknowledgement/dismissal and Company-side assignment/ownership are **Founder-deferred,
valuable-but-not-required**. Saved views/annotations are **optional, no package**. Task and follow-up remain with
`007`/`034`. Metric→evidence drill-down retains partial ownership and is constrained by the N≥10 privacy boundary —
**a constraint, not a defect**. Only **`KORA-WP-144`** was allocated, and only because the Founder Target Platform
requires the Product to answer *"what changed?"*.

### AN.8 — Product Experience Definition of Done

Programme completion requires: the four system foundations implemented **and demonstrated on real canonical
surfaces** · typography drift eliminated on the canonical Product · **no canonical text below the Benchmark floor** ·
spacing system adopted and uncontrolled drift eliminated · every canonical route carrying an archetype · surface and
state grammar in use with the seven states distinct · **Privacy Boundary quality preserved** · the six threshold
metrics encoding significance · mobile within the AN.1 calibrated contracts · navigation reflecting Product jobs ·
**no internal roadmap language on customer-facing surfaces** · **`/company` a real Executive Cockpit** · Company
Tier A/B passing Gates A–I · Admin, Advisor and Worker passing applicable gates · Partner passing after capability ·
**explicit Founder Visual Acceptance on every Tier-A surface** · enforcement preventing regression · and
**`KORA-WP-144` delivered before the Founder Target Platform is declared complete**.

**Product Experience complete is NOT Production ready.** They are different conditions on different gates.


### AN.9 — PX Wave 1 canonical inputs — FROZEN 2026-09-25 (report `268`, Founder ratification)

Benchmark V2 remains recorded **by reference** and is **not rewritten**. `KORA-WP-125` remains **COMPLETE and not
reopened**. This subsection adds no design decision: it makes durable the inputs Benchmark V2 already ratified, which
until now existed only in governance lineage and therefore could not be cited by implementation or acceptance.

**Durable artifact:** `docs/BENCHMARK_V2_CANONICAL_GRAMMAR.md` — a tracked repository file, citable by
`KORA-WP-139` and `KORA-WP-140` during implementation and at acceptance. It carries, and is the single source for:

- the **nine** canonical surface roles — Hero Judgment, Primary Metric, Supporting Metric, Evidence Panel,
  Warning / Safeguard, Action, Disclosure, Suppressed / Boundary, Operational Row. **No tenth role.**
- the **seven** canonical states — ZERO, NO DATA, INSUFFICIENT DATA, SUPPRESSED, NOT YET AVAILABLE, LOADING, ERROR.
  **No eighth state.**
- the structural invariants `ZERO != NO DATA`, `SUPPRESSED != ERROR`, `NOT YET AVAILABLE != ERROR`.
- the exact nine-step implementation type scale, with desktop and mobile values, line heights, weight and tracking
  intent, and a per-step mapping to the WP-124 source.
- the one-family plan: Plus Jakarta Sans retained; Instrument Serif, Playfair Display and Hanken Grotesk removed.

**Typography precedence, ratified.** `design/wp124-final/kora-signal.css` remains normative for **colour** — `PX`
values are asserted verbatim against it and that is unchanged. It is **not** normative for typographic values:
Benchmark V2 was ratified later, specifically because `KORA-WP-125`'s quality bar was insufficient, and **a historical
normative implementation does not override a later quality bar**. Where the two disagree on a type value, Benchmark V2
governs. 13px body text is **not** preserved for historical compatibility. Verdict on the WP-124 role model:
**PARTIALLY REUSE** — taxonomy and naming architecture reused, numeric values replaced, class-based CSS mechanism not
reused (it is not imported by the Product and its classes have zero Product uses), transcription discipline reused.

**Privacy Boundary preserved.** `components/privacy/PrivacyBoundaryNotice.tsx` is the reference implementation for the
Suppressed / Boundary role and must not be regressed. Its `role="status"` is correct: a boundary is a state of the
world, not a fault.

**Demonstrator interpretation — evidence may satisfy proof where the contract permits it.** Code truth at `ac91cfa`:
`/advisor/companies/[assignmentId]` already adopts **eight** distinct shared roles and carries **no** text below the
11px floor, so `KORA-WP-140` clause (F) is satisfied there at baseline and `KORA-WP-139` clause (E) needs no change
there. **That surface is recorded as a conforming demonstrator by evidence, not by mutation** — churn introduced
solely so a package can claim to have touched it is a Benchmark V2 violation, not acceptance. `/company/reports` is
recorded as the true Wave 1 serialization surface and carries essentially the whole demonstrator load for both
packages: 31 of 31 sub-floor occurrences, zero shared-role adoption, 17 hand-rolled containers.

Registry Section `AN.3` and the Section C rows name this demonstrator `/advisor/companies/[id]`; the real path is
`/advisor/companies/[assignmentId]`. **Same surface, and the route must not be renamed to match the prose.**
Acceptance evidence cites the real path.

**`KORA-WP-047` accessibility lock — the rule.** `tests/unit/kora-wp-047-design-system-a11y.test.ts` asserts the exact
source string `role={variant === 'access-denied' ? 'alert' : undefined}` in `components/ui/EmptyState.tsx`.
`KORA-WP-140` **keeps that behaviour byte-compatible** and introduces **SUPPRESSED as a structurally distinct typed
state that must not masquerade as access-denied or as an alert**. `access-denied` continues to mean an authorization
failure, for which `role="alert"` is correct. **The `KORA-WP-047` assertion is not to be superseded for convenience**;
supersession requires its own recorded rationale, as the `B147` precedent did.

**`PxTone` finding — CLOSED as FALSE POSITIVE / TYPE-SAFE BY DESIGN.** Report `268` flagged two call sites in
`app/admin/advisor-governance/page.tsx` passing `tone='mute'` and `tone='ink'` as outside the `PxTone` keys. They are
not `PxTone` consumers: they render `Metric`, whose own contract declares `tone?: 'ink' | 'mute'`. Two distinct `tone`
props were conflated by the audit sweep. `tsc --noEmit` passes clean at `ac91cfa`, which independently confirms no
union violation exists. **No defect, no implementation input, no package.**

### AN.10 — EXECUTABLE ACCEPTANCE AUTHORITY — authority repair 2026-09-27 (report `277`, Founder decision)

**The defect.** DELTA 5 (report `266`, commit `1151ab2`) amended `KORA-WP-127`, `128`, `129`, `131` and `143` to
accept against "**Benchmark V2 Gates A–I**". Section `AN` records Benchmark V2 "by reference, never duplication", and
that reference resolves to a governance lineage — Product Experience Quality Audit → Benchmark V2 → Page-by-Page Audit
→ Implementation Program — which was **never versioned**. The normative A–I enumeration therefore has no
repository-resident definition and cannot be objectively evaluated. It blocked **closure**, never execution.

**Forensic basis — CASE 3, AUTHORITY NEVER VERSIONED** (report `277`). Read-only history forensics across every local
and remote ref: the strings "Gate C" and "Gate H" have **never appeared in any tracked blob, on any ref, at any point
in this repository's history**, so a nine-item A–I enumeration cannot ever have been committed; no commit has ever
contained both "Gate D" and "Gate H". Every other "Gate ⟨letter⟩" occurrence is independently accounted for as
unrelated — `Gate DPO`, `Gate Factor`, `Eligibility Gate Engine`, `Gate G-3`/`G-4`, `Gate Affected`,
`KORA-WP-007`'s own `Founder Gate A`/`B`, `KORA-WP-124`'s package-local `Gate I`, and the `(A)`–`(J)` acceptance
letters of `138`/`143`. Commit `1151ab2` — the commit that **ratified** Benchmark V2 — changed exactly three files and
committed no gate text.

**Founder decision, 2026-09-27 — REPAIR MODEL C, prospective.** The unexecutable reference is replaced, **for future
execution and acceptance only**, by the executable acceptance taxonomy this registry already carries.

**No historical equivalence is asserted.** This registry does **not** state that "Gates A–I" were the `KORA-WP-126`
taxonomy, does **not** state that the phrase was a naming error, and does **not** supply a retrospective A–I
definition. There is no evidence for any of those claims and none is manufactured here — **17 criteria ≠ 9 gates, and
no mapping between them exists.** The historical wording is **retained wherever it appears**, including this
section's own by-reference scope list, which remains the record of what Benchmark V2 ratification covered, and
including the DELTA 5 adjudication rationale and the `M`-series milestone conditions.

**Executable acceptance authority — referenced, never duplicated.** It is the criteria taxonomy stated verbatim in
this registry's own **`KORA-WP-126` entry**: the mechanically enforceable checks that entry declares it "now owns"
(typography tokens/scale/floor · spacing token rules · route archetype declaration · surface-role constraints where
mechanically safe · the seven-state resolution contract · page-length and mobile-ratio measurement · navigation
budget/label/internal-state rules · the no-bare-threshold-metric contract), together with that entry's binding
separation — **hierarchy, scanability, actionability and craft are REVIEW-ENFORCED; Logo-Off, premium moments and
final visual acceptance are FOUNDER JUDGMENT**, and neither class may be given fragile automated tests. **No criterion
is added, removed, reworded or reinterpreted by this repair, and no nine-gate mapping is created.** No competing
source of truth is introduced: this subsection points at existing text and nothing is copied.

**Resolution rule.** Every occurrence of "Benchmark V2 Gates A–I" in this registry — package acceptance clauses,
`M`-series milestone conditions and `AN.8` alike — resolves **prospectively** to the authority named above. Where the
phrase records history or reasoning it is preserved unchanged and carries no executable force.

**Authority layers, kept distinct.**

| layer | authority |
|---|---|
| **Historical** | Benchmark V2 ratification and its preserved governance lineage (this section; report `266`) |
| **Executable acceptance** | the `KORA-WP-126` taxonomy above |
| **Mechanical implementation** | `lib/px-acceptance/*` and the COMPLETE `139`–`142` systems, every threshold delegated to its owning package and defined nowhere twice |
| **Founder judgment** | exactly the criteria `assertMechanisable()` deliberately refuses to automate |

**`KORA-WP-126` is an instrument, not an acceptor.** It never replaces Founder Review, and the **Founder Review
Publication Contract (Section A) remains independently binding**.

**`KORA-WP-131` ownership is unchanged.** It **consumes** this taxonomy as the final cross-environment gate; it does

**`AN.9` secondary gap — CLASS A, HISTORICAL EVIDENCE GAP ONLY.** `AN.9` cites
